%beginitem

    print("f", end="");
    return n

print(f(9) and f(8))
%code

%beginitem

%code
def g(a,b):
    c=85
    if b>=1:
        c=9
    elif b==-5:
         return 1
    else: 
        return 8
    return c

print(g(-9,-9))
%code
%enditem

    c=4
    return a+b+c

print(g(b),a,b,c)

%enditem

%beginitem
13. Що буде виведено за виконання?
Повідомлення інтерпретатора про помилку позначати error.

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2

try:
    print(f(0) or f(1), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code

    c=16
    if b<-3:
        c=0
    if a<=-4:
         c=5
    else: 
        return 3
    return c

print(h(3,9))

    global c
    a=5
    b=4
    c=2
    return a+b+c

a,b,c=8,1,7
print(g(b),a,b,c)
%code
%enditem

    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2

try:
    print(f(0), end="")
    print(f(1), end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")


%code
%enditem
\end {large}}
%end
%end

        return int(1 / x)

        return 0

    finally:

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        print(int(1 / x),
                   end="")
    except:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        raise TypeError
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    f(0)
    f(1)
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    print(f(1) or f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

    c=36
    if a:

    return c

def f(x):
    try:
        print(int(1 / x),
                   end="")
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
    finally:
        print(3, end="")


try:
    f(1)
    f(0)
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2

try:
    print(f(1) or f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")    

%code

    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except:
        print(1, end="")
        return 0
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 1
    finally:
        print(3, end="")


try:
    print(f(1) and f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%beginitem
5. Що буде виведено за виконання (повідомлення інтерпретатора про помилку позначати error)?

%code
def f(a,b):
    c=94
    if a!=5:
        c=0
    if a<=-5:
         return 3
    else: 
        return 8
    return c

print(f(3,5))
%code
%enditem

13. Що буде виведено за виконання?
Повідомлення інтерпретатора про помилку позначати error.

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    print(f(0), end="")
    print(f(1), end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
%enditem
\end {large}}
%end

    if d<=-4: 
        return 9
    if d>=1:
         y=6
    else:
         y=2
    return y

print(h(9))
%code
%enditem

6. Що буде виведено за виконання (повідомлення інтерпретатора про помилку позначати error)?

%code
a,b,c=6,1,3
def h(a):
    global c
    a=1
    b-=3
    c=4
    return a+b+c

a,b,c=9,0,5
print(h(a),a,b,c)
%code
%enditem

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        print(int(1 / x),
                   end="")
    except:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        raise TypeError
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    f(1)
    f(0)
except:
    print(4, end="")

%enditem
\end {large}}
%end

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except:
        print(1, end="")
        return 0
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 1
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2


try:
    print(f(1) and f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

    v=93
    if b>-2: 
        v=8
    if b!=-3:
         return 3
    else:
         v=8
    return v

    global c

    b=1
    c=2
    return a+b+c

a,b,c=8,6,9
print(h(b),a,b,c)
%code
%enditem

    for el in s:
        el+=1
    return s

s=[1,2,3]
s=f(s)
print('R', end=' ')
print(s)
%code

def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    print(f(0), end="")
    print(f(1), end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2

try:
    print(f(0), end="")
    print(f(1), end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")


%code

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    print(f(1) or f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
%enditem
\end {large}}
%end

def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2

try:
    print(f(0), end="")
    print(f(1), end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
def g(a,b):
    c=71
    if a:
        c=4
    elif b>=1:
         c=0
    else: 
        return 5
    return c

print(g(-5,8))
%code
%enditem

    global c
    a=2
    b+=4
    c=1
    return a+b+c

a,b,c=3,6,8
print(g(a),a,b,c)
%code

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")

try:
    print(f(1) or f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except:
        print(1, end="")
        return 0
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 1
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2


try:
    print(f(1) and f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except TypeError:
        print(1, end="")
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 0
    finally:
        print(3, end="")
        return 2

try:
    print(f(0) or f(1), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")

Повідомлення інтерпретатора про помилку позначати error.

%code
def f(x):
    try:
        return int(1 / x)
    except:
        print(1, end="")
        return 0
    else:
        print(2, end="")
        return 1
    finally:
        print(3, end="")


try:
    print(f(1) and f(0), end="")
    print(3, end="")
except:
    print(4, end="")
